##### These are the campaigns of our lives, the kind of creativity that built brands this year. Happy New Year!

This newsletter is sponsored by our friends at [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/976b2e89-5486-4e2c-b1bc-c3d450662f00?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), the always-on, affordable brand tracking dashboard. Now tracking 25 markets (and growing), giving you consistent brand health data everywhere you operate.

* What is a [K-shaped](https://substack.com/redirect/1cea4c40-b9b6-40ad-a344-045caf7de707?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) economy?
* Syria's [newly issued currency](https://substack.com/redirect/31131808-93bd-4b95-895a-0922f383e2a9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) combines national symbolism [with](https://substack.com/redirect/84807313-2d94-498d-a795-a1a9f39c9a45?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) beautiful visual design.

*In partnership with [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/976b2e89-5486-4e2c-b1bc-c3d450662f00?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)*

##### **The Bimma x Tracksuit: [2025 Collab of the Year Report](https://substack.com/redirect/b88f515e-dfe9-47d2-bddb-607fd7c4d4c5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is finally here!**

For the first time, [Bimma Williams](https://substack.com/redirect/d8bd5c87-1704-4bff-a797-94a80e9b6e3b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) partnered with Tracksuit to measure sneaker collaborations the way consumers actually experience them, with real data and real cultural insight. They [analyzed](https://substack.com/redirect/b88f515e-dfe9-47d2-bddb-607fd7c4d4c5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the drops that moved people, shifted conversations, and built real brand energy. 

And built the [COLLAB Index](https://substack.com/redirect/b88f515e-dfe9-47d2-bddb-607fd7c4d4c5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): an evaluation tool that assesses collaborations across six dimensions: Chemistry, Originality, Legacy, Leadership, Audience Engagement, and Brand Energy.

Most brands still pick collaborators based on vibes, but vibes alone don’t sell anything. The [COLLAB Index](https://substack.com/redirect/b88f515e-dfe9-47d2-bddb-607fd7c4d4c5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) blends two data sources into one clear score that shows what worked *and* why:

* Consumer Reality: Tracksuit data from a survey of 2,000+ qualified US participants.
* Cultural Reality: Evaluation from Bimma’s network of creators, journalists and strategists

> *It’s the simplest way for marketers to understand, and prove, what makes a collaboration truly effective. If you’re planning a brand collab in 2026, don’t pitch it without this [Collab of the Year](https://substack.com/redirect/b88f515e-dfe9-47d2-bddb-607fd7c4d4c5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) report.*

* Can AI Chatbots run ads [without](https://substack.com/redirect/5f131e2a-d09f-43fc-8542-face3ab0907b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) losing consumer trust?
* Berlin power outage hits [45,000 homes](https://substack.com/redirect/ed642588-b726-4124-b460-ce283551263f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after suspected arson attack.
* [CMOs](https://substack.com/redirect/b8c38923-5c68-425b-af55-1df8cac85273?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), don’t believe AI’s big lie.
* Tracksuit and Bimma Williams unveil [2025 Collab of the Year Report](https://substack.com/redirect/b88f515e-dfe9-47d2-bddb-607fd7c4d4c5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).\*\*
* Microsoft CEO Satya Nadella is now [blogging](https://substack.com/redirect/37fde557-58b0-40a3-ae72-b5d800c41687?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) about AI slop.
	+ More than [20% of YouTube’s feed](https://substack.com/redirect/4d0a6796-2c67-4d54-a4b3-1110b3c99c75?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is now “AI slop,” report finds.
* [Clicks](https://substack.com/redirect/3e0d0a20-81c5-4d53-93c1-2a8c659f6b77?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is bringing its first smartphone and a new keyboard to CES 2026.
* MTV’s [music-only channels](https://substack.com/redirect/00973b4b-6927-49c6-a0f0-dae9e279ee75?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to go off the air.
* More and more men want to be pegged, according to [Feeld](https://substack.com/redirect/b4dccc1d-95cd-4f92-87ba-cdafbbb393be?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* The New Surveillance State Is [You](https://substack.com/redirect/76ffa420-36db-4bf7-a25b-53c59ee420f3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* [kirkification](https://substack.com/redirect/94fe27b8-5cbf-4e3a-a63c-be7ef5e026ac?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): stealing a face.
* The ‘[Exciting Business Opportunity](https://substack.com/redirect/3b76dfc5-4df5-4211-b167-31d154699c1b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ That Ruined Our Lives.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!c5py!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F01163944-8286-4d66-9091-cb2ea5412c62_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/402acfb3-7761-45e7-9486-1fc58b21fa52?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)These are the best brand-building, creatively marvellous, you-can’t-hate-what-works campaigns of 2025. We’re going month by month, with one short sentence of review or award per campaign.

##### January 2025

* Australia’s 2025 Lamb ad campaign ‘[The Comments Section](https://substack.com/redirect/ac904071-a739-4cd3-aaef-7e5f966e35e5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ was great but not better than 2024 ‘The Generational Gap’ ad spot.
	+ Best use of the internet’s weird, quirky, and annoying habits in a campaign.
* Dove’s yearlong “[Unready for Anything](https://substack.com/redirect/4bdcfb29-3e80-4118-906a-38d98fff930d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” campaign.
	+ Best use of a major event, but it’s a shame it wasn’t pushed more heavily to chronically online audiences.
* It’s Brick In NYC: Nigel Sylvester and [Jordan](https://substack.com/redirect/857c0322-647c-4656-94be-bec44e689313?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) unveil Bike Air Truck activation.
	+ Possibly one of my favourite Jordan collaborations of the year, Nigel remains a great collaborator and brings out the best in almost all brands.
* Britannia [redefines](https://substack.com/redirect/bf17d23a-c294-4e83-bdbc-47227216d78e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) advertising with tree-shaped billboards, earns praise: ‘Love this initiative’
	+ A good sustainability-focused campaign, which wasn’t particularly sustainable, but hey, you see the leaves poking out of the billboard.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!-Ifc!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F0b0b7b5f-08f2-459c-b6aa-4a3d41661e9e_5398x2627.jpeg)](https://substack.com/redirect/e7658eac-4d6c-4828-9193-1942beca7be7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Coors Light “[Case of the Mondays](https://substack.com/redirect/a8a07eb5-9a90-43b5-8965-95cbcbd867ef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” typo campaign.
	+ Mischief US is locked in when it comes to using local media for brand campaigns like this.
* Harrison Ford [partnering](https://substack.com/redirect/95165149-35a0-438c-8f0a-0847358149ba?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with Glenmorangie Whisky for an iconic Scottish ad campaign.
	+ Can’t wait for Shrinking Season 3.
* '[Actually, it's IKEA](https://substack.com/redirect/ac63cc8c-0a9a-40f9-8145-0d4e177a1587?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)' campaign from Rethink. 
	+ Possibly my favourite brand platform of the year, the slogan works so well that, even if the campaign takes itself too seriously, the slogan makes it work.
* Heinz [triggers](https://substack.com/redirect/c30c0e12-13c9-4168-9feb-65b82ccb1abb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Britain’s taste memories with logo-less ad spot.
	+ Don’t go logo-less without brand heritage or assets, it has to be logical.
* Hellmann’s [hits](https://substack.com/redirect/58a41135-9966-4552-8085-d5b56fa5aba6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the Super Bowl spot with ‘When Harry met Sally’ remake.
	+ IP BABY!!!
* [Sarah Lancashire](https://substack.com/redirect/4ccd499c-d64e-48da-b850-d361d85ec131?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) takes up case of Yorkshire Tea crime.
	+ It’s funny, and Brits don’t play about their tea.
* Apple TV’s [Severance pop-up](https://substack.com/redirect/b0db8182-c123-44a9-9733-78850c8f56d5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in Grand Central. 
	+ More brand pop-ups at museums and train stations are coming.

Honourable mentions: Nike ‘[Year of the Mamba](https://substack.com/redirect/8616cdc3-b48f-4466-b3c8-1f72aba25ffc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g),’ John Cena x McDonald’s [McValue](https://substack.com/redirect/7f27fa61-26a8-428a-8b95-c5b9102b1c17?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) campaign, and [Progresso](https://substack.com/redirect/e7be6e21-a0d1-45d3-ae19-7f35da001cf7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Soup Drops. 

##### February 2025

* The Cadbury Dairy Milk’ [Made to Share](https://substack.com/redirect/0c79378d-776b-42f5-9029-ed236f4ddd76?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ campaign.
	+ More popular than Coke’s relaunched “Share a Coke” campaign.
* Clarks celebrating 200 years with global launch of ‘From Somerset to the World’ documentary.
	+ Narrated by Yasiin Bey, it’s a delight to watch.
* Kellogg’s “[See you in the morning](https://substack.com/redirect/b743a161-ba0b-4a3e-942a-e07b6cc47d46?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” by Leo Burnett UK.
	+ Can I get more OOH ads with slogans and brand characters in focus?
* [United Airlines](https://substack.com/redirect/89c5edb0-7bb6-4140-bc0e-756b4b38f9b3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Kendrick Lamar Super Bowl performance tweet. 
	+ Best brand tweet of 2025?
* IKEA Canada’s “[U UP?](https://substack.com/redirect/0ad9c14b-148f-40d1-9492-65621a3a0d66?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” comms campaign. 
	+ Brands experimenting with “social” features to be social is rare.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!RtWd!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fad7f67aa-2df9-46f4-adcf-861fb8897cd9_1187x862.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/58a411fe-27d0-43e1-9e5c-ec335c7db9a7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* On Footwear’s “[Soft Wins](https://substack.com/redirect/89c6da07-3444-46b3-996c-8ddaaff5d48a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” campaign with Elmo and Roger Federer.
	+ Most neutral campaign of the year, Switzerland.
* [Chili’s](https://substack.com/redirect/1ca1afab-b6e7-413e-ad22-079c3cccb383?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) makes a Lifetime Holiday movie for National Margarita Day.
	+ It pays to be early, as the rest of the year was filled with either parodies or brand short films.
* Angel Soft offering a ‘[bathroom break](https://substack.com/redirect/445bb061-b64e-40b0-be4c-d07ce8fc2662?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ in their Super Bowl ad.
	+ Timely.
* CeraVe hiring [Anthony Davis](https://substack.com/redirect/37afefff-4e01-4bf6-8e3a-4ee1621eb251?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as Head of CeraVe for new campaign. 
	+ CeraVe knows how to tease a campaign.
* StreetEasy’s “[Never Become a Former New Yorker](https://substack.com/redirect/d7b838ed-d445-42c3-9a98-d32cbf69070a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” campaign. 
	+ Never and Former work like a charm to scare people and make them rethink their decision.
* Tesco’s [OOH campaign](https://substack.com/redirect/992bcc70-22d5-4ca1-9eeb-64d30f6d70b2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) uses shopping bags and veggies to deliver messages.
	+ Another request, please use your shopping bags in campaigns.
* Choreography by [Nick Ball](https://substack.com/redirect/5c082d6c-8a70-4d38-abbe-03de6bf63f83?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is the star of DoorDash Canada’s new ad campaign.
	+ GAP x Katseye before we got GAP x Katseye.

Honourable mentions: Knorr x [#DeliverBetter](https://substack.com/redirect/8847d58a-039a-42c7-b5e3-b7286ba2d8db?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) campaign, Burberry bringing its knight to life with [cross-stitch campaign](https://substack.com/redirect/220ce5bf-ce09-408b-a554-9fb07c066bb0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), and Allbirds x [Stanley Tucci](https://substack.com/redirect/4f109301-3194-46ae-bb59-ccd55f92bc0f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) content series. 

##### March 2025

* Corona turns [rush hour](https://substack.com/redirect/83d95e27-17bf-4140-873d-90955093dc4e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) into a moment of calm with sunset broadcast.
	+ Heineken sells an agenda, Corona sells a vibe.
* Marks & Spencer’s “[Only 1 ingredient](https://substack.com/redirect/9423f5c4-85ab-450e-abe7-d22f19794752?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” cornflakes and other [cereal products](https://substack.com/redirect/32224933-769d-483f-8140-56b11f267529?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) went [viral](https://substack.com/redirect/9c8338a4-2fc7-46d8-8b59-86ff180e80f9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for [too honest](https://substack.com/redirect/e526ca92-d78c-4af1-a657-a29cf0430487?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) packaging.
	+ MAHA, aesthetics, UK, and brand edition.
* Visit Norway [collaborated](https://substack.com/redirect/2db26fc5-fb52-4a45-8a7c-b35837a2321e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with “This is Yvonne” from Thon Hotel Ålesund.
	+ This is authentic.
* [KFC](https://substack.com/redirect/de7ae942-57f6-41e2-a920-ed2a423f00f1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) embraces the weird and wonderful with ‘All Hail Gravy’
	+ Controversial, but gooood, perfect for 2025
* Women’s Aid UK [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/b9495a4e-6b70-43c0-9493-f75bf6f1c17b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) “The Monster Who Came to Tea” to raise funding for domestic abuse support services.
	+ Emotional storytelling and illustrations.
* ‘[Awkboards](https://substack.com/redirect/fb387916-f82f-459f-ae62-452eb2bb5392?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)‘, via FCB Aotearoa, NZ spotlight when products like laxatives and lubricant are ordered through Uber Eats.
	+ More awkward words on billboards, please!

[![No alternative text description for this image](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!gpQv!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe0693ebb-3ba5-446d-a4c9-ad1b0fc69d73_800x330.jpeg "No alternative text description for this image")](https://substack.com/redirect/9117c26a-e50a-42d2-aceb-ee013d0b2813?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* What’s up with [The Ordinary](https://substack.com/redirect/f342c07d-de51-4082-999e-fde95c0cc9d1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) selling eggs?
	+ Everything is Marketing.
* B&Q and Leo Burnett hammer home affordability with new ‘[Prices Nailed](https://substack.com/redirect/1ae234b2-9140-484c-8530-78b3927e16c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ campaign.
	+ Price tags on billboards in 2025: bold, and could be a trend.
* DICK’S Sporting Goods [taps](https://substack.com/redirect/ce05d3a4-b4c5-4aaa-87b5-de171d5de2bf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) IShowSpeed, Tom Brady, Kevin Durant, Suni Lee and Travis Hunter for latest Footwear campaign.
	+ Should’ve saved this campaign for FIFA World Cup.
* Maple Leaf Foods [hits back](https://substack.com/redirect/844fc548-2020-4b74-958e-78bbabc34692?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) at U.S. tariffs by urging customers to buy Canadian, even from its rivals, simply look for the leaf as a sign to confirm the Canadian roots.
	+ The best use of a brand asset and rivalry.
* PUMA’s “Go Wild” ad campaign [celebrates](https://substack.com/redirect/6820780d-ae5e-4c91-931c-09031ea78726?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the Runner’s High.
	+ Best use of an old, iconic song.
* Mercedes-Benz [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/f220b1a8-e4dc-4c90-ba33-7f07abada8e2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) ‘Class of Creators’ project [with](https://substack.com/redirect/dcf24071-caa6-4c82-8ab1-fdad55ee351d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Ice Spice, KidSuper, Gustaf Westman.
	+ Experimentation in this category is good.

Honourable mentions: Moët & Chandon’s [champagne cork](https://substack.com/redirect/1a7bc7d8-8011-4511-a820-2d1e5d40b254?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) balloon, [Kylie Kelce](https://substack.com/redirect/f3e168d6-9db7-4220-a8b7-18ee3a68f7fe?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) x Liquid Death, and Volkswagen and [Kong](https://substack.com/redirect/d2531715-a1d1-4ee0-8c0f-d91cbb125fb7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) collab on Dog Toy.

##### April 2025

* People are [surprised](https://substack.com/redirect/57e2000a-ab0a-4171-9fca-f7cb4cccc633?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) JCPenney is trendy. Its comeback ad campaign “Yes, JCPenney” challenges that.
	+ Another brand platform that just makes sense, as a lost brand finds its cool again.
* Goodyear's “[STILL](https://substack.com/redirect/dd924f54-7064-406d-bc3b-9eb826ba63ff?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” ad campaign celebrating the role of Goodyear tires in American culture through the years.
	+ Best editing campaign of the year.
* Heineken [unveils](https://substack.com/redirect/100349f4-4d58-4d04-908a-60b4bec94181?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) a phone case that flips your phone over when it hears “Cheers’’!
	+ Socialisation as a service.
* Progressive, Auto Insurance brand says [drive safely](https://substack.com/redirect/f1491a23-258c-4ad7-8443-7bbcc560c2a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) around Calvin Klein’s Bad Bunny billboards.
	+ LED truck advertising hits its peak again and again.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Tw7M!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F7a8de6b8-ea8b-469a-a8a9-0c65251441a1_1200x675.jpeg)](https://substack.com/redirect/c59b134a-2a67-4218-a2b0-731da9cff363?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Sanpellegrino’s new campaign “[CIAO!](https://substack.com/redirect/903a0802-0e6e-4e8f-b8e9-cac34569db4e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” starring Michael Imperioli and Steve Schirripa from The Sopranos is beautifully executed.
	+ Best celebrity duo campaign of the year.
* Billie’s NYC ‘[scratch and sniff](https://substack.com/redirect/a13134f4-4ca5-419c-9195-880802138d40?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ campaign invites you to smell giant armpits on the billboard.
	+ New Trend, [Subway](https://substack.com/redirect/537db9f6-3211-4434-b91f-6dc7ca14a058?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Stella Artois [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/85d19825-2371-4567-9ac9-e9c907057548?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) OOH campaign that humorously nods to the universal experience of navigating a crowded bar.
	+ Saving the beer, a universal experience.
* La Vie launches Collaborative Campaign, allowing other vegan brands to [repurpose](https://substack.com/redirect/f653b081-7d07-4c8c-9d10-aee50c2d02cd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) their ad.
	+ So-so ad, but a good strategy.
* Harbin Beer’s “[Back Rack Hack](https://substack.com/redirect/253e7cd2-4913-4e86-9138-7de27660ca38?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” campaign. 
	+ Product positioning in real life turns into a campaign.
* KitKat [replaces](https://substack.com/redirect/9880640c-2399-4ed4-96a3-bf3c8f3d382c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) phones with KitKat Bars in latest campaign.
	+ It’s going to be a big year for people holding not-phone phones.
* PETA UK [encourages](https://substack.com/redirect/42f15943-e73e-406c-b284-1047009f3918?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) people to have a vegan diet for Good Sh\*t.
	+ That’s some good sh\*t.
* The [Kid with Crocs](https://substack.com/redirect/70b480b5-4bec-4755-8ea0-65ccfabf17a2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) project came to an end with revelation about the whole project being supported by Crocs. 
	+ It was perfect, but damn, are we in an age of not disclosing sponsors?

Honourable mentions: Autistica’s “[See us as individuals, not stereotypes](https://substack.com/redirect/366887c4-b190-464f-a17d-235dda273552?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g),” Specsavers temporarily rebrands as ‘[Specsandhearingsavers](https://substack.com/redirect/c71113a1-ba45-495d-b517-3d09957eb268?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g),” and BritBox’s [14-hours-long one-shot](https://substack.com/redirect/5f656ae4-5c53-4bb1-8069-67b4674874c3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) take.

##### May 2025

* IKEA UK started a series called “[Life in Stitches](https://substack.com/redirect/53c380fe-9797-4a8b-a687-19f304fdc389?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).”
	+ Best social media content series of the year.
* ‘[A’ja Wilson’s on top](https://substack.com/redirect/83f79943-c66f-4200-a636-180fbd2e6463?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ in brilliant Nike ad by [Malia Obama](https://substack.com/redirect/42f074c5-9974-4b95-81de-4a911b51f006?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), storytelling and branding in this campaign is at its best.
	+ Creative Direction is so gooood.
* Human Taxidermy and Witches? Nah, It’s just a teaser for [Slate EVs](https://substack.com/redirect/55879763-4b09-45f5-bce6-f6c0bb918e32?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ IP on the hero product, baby!!
* Credit Bank of Peru’s ‘[Guardians of the ATM](https://substack.com/redirect/f45f853d-1818-431d-88d6-0f25d67e20c5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ campaign turns caution into a message of trust.
	+ Don’t just say trust, show and depict trust.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!4KxZ!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fcfe3c2e9-e2a5-429d-9d12-0af900a23d1b_960x540.jpeg)](https://substack.com/redirect/551f113f-8437-4088-9147-7951d2a85b37?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* VML’s campaign for Colgate, “[Tous les sourires ne commencent pas par un sourire](https://substack.com/redirect/629f4465-5630-4de7-8d9c-5d1b0b585a75?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)“ (”Not Every Smile Starts as a Smile”).
	+ Best Kids campaign of 2025.
* O Boticário’s [Mother’s Day ad](https://substack.com/redirect/cea003cc-387a-4796-b313-a08064e3fb51?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) looks at the silent struggle of trying to conceive.
	+ Brand doing the best emotional long-form campaigns.
* MullenLowe Istanbul bring us the [sweetness of summertime in telescopic](https://substack.com/redirect/7caacdd5-e15f-4b2e-b656-00d5602b5bb9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) new campaign for Cornetto.
	+ Look deeper and further into design of your product, you will find a new campaign.
* Ocado’s ad starts with the [final frame](https://substack.com/redirect/01beb1b9-3e01-4565-8be9-5256a731bc17?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), then runs in reverse.
	+ It’s a good insight into doing BTS.
* Toblerone highlights the struggles of eating … Toblerone in ‘[Chocolate Like Nobody’s Watching](https://substack.com/redirect/534a304b-221b-4acb-baa2-d7a5ac2b89f4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)‘ global campaign.
	+ Incredible use of product design and an obsession with humouring the audience.
* Vogel’s send Kiwis [a slice of home](https://substack.com/redirect/dd64c7dc-b18f-4da8-b346-252320fba9d5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in new ‘Toastcards’ campaign via DDB Aotearoa.
	+ Nostalgia with a hint of creative experimentation.
* Theo James and Vittoria Ceretti in [Dolce & Gabbana](https://substack.com/redirect/655a5228-7c77-4cbf-9cf4-25fdebb07c77?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) ad campaign.
	+ Best sex appeal and most charming campaign of the year.
* Oura's “[Give Us the Finger](https://substack.com/redirect/fbd9207a-36c2-4395-9e13-b725e59cef2e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” campaign.
	+ Old people rock: Old and Oura.

Honourable mentions: Mascot NYC’s “[The best thing to sit on your face](https://substack.com/redirect/50c09f8c-4b96-4a6b-bbac-e07e0cfcf4b9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” OOH campaign, Bottega Veneta’s “[Hands](https://substack.com/redirect/8e45ed4f-85ba-4db8-b62d-2bb55ead55d9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” ad, and Curry’s “[Beyond Techspectations](https://substack.com/redirect/d7155848-fdb9-4069-bd49-80b54f652095?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” (accessibility edition).

##### June 2025

* Channel 4’s public apology campaign “[Sorry. Not sorry](https://substack.com/redirect/45c3ccb5-8267-437b-92bf-8895d827948a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” – It's not backing down on diversity, equity and inclusion (DEI).
	+ Bold and literal.
* Samuel L. Jackson opens [new chapter](https://substack.com/redirect/b3cf449a-a3bc-467f-b1c8-7f0f1a3c6944?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for adidas Superstar sneakerline.
	+ Best narrator-focused campaign of the year.
* Greenpeace uses OOH ad space to call out [protest policing](https://substack.com/redirect/09e071d4-5170-4523-803e-9200227b51a8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in the UK.
	+ Great use of public ad space by making it more public-focused.
* Superman soars 1,000ft over [The Shard](https://substack.com/redirect/b4b9bbee-896e-4f9a-820b-2000a3bb63d8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to promote Hollywood blockbuster.
	+ Movie Marketing reinvents early 2010s.

[![Superman Soars Above London in First-Ever Skyline Sculpture at The Shard](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!vTE4!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F27a9ec69-b59b-40f7-aae2-5907c23172e6_1080x684.jpeg "Superman Soars Above London in First-Ever Skyline Sculpture at The Shard")](https://substack.com/redirect/113f9344-45b7-4731-8d5e-269d0273f39d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* [SCARY GOOD:](https://substack.com/redirect/802790b8-7d41-44b2-a5a2-2d15074a9899?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Nike Football’s new brand identity.
	+ Best anti-hero campaign of the year.
* Fortnum & Mason joined forces with [Martin Parr](https://substack.com/redirect/98508593-9870-4b7c-a928-2a541973917d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for new campaign.
	+ RIP to a legend, this campaign was perfect, best of the summer.
* Canva’s OOH brand takeover at Waterloo Station in [London](https://substack.com/redirect/a7509e0b-742b-4d66-97d2-9a9de2e4ec47?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Nice use of annoying client design suggestions.
* Brittany Ferries creates ripples with ‘[Wake](https://substack.com/redirect/8fdb176b-7262-4ccc-ae7a-96793e7c58aa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)‘ OOH campaign.
	+ Typography as storytelling.
* Cinnamon Toast Crunch’s latest campaign, ‘[Must Cinnadust](https://substack.com/redirect/268391fd-52db-42b6-bbae-ec305c6364f6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).’
	+ Best product as anti-hero campaign of the year.

Honourable mentions: Run-D.M.C.’s ‘[It’s Like That](https://substack.com/redirect/3417561d-603b-4415-ad61-dadebfecf60e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ reimagined by LEGO & Global Girls Crew, Burberry [vinyl print](https://substack.com/redirect/2f9a7d1f-2bb3-4952-8690-1513eb7dc056?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) social advert, and Noah’s [ballsy](https://substack.com/redirect/bfb7a809-c84e-4202-b70d-4d5708fc9e29?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) campaign.

##### July 2025

* Axe and Lola MullenLowe [launch](https://substack.com/redirect/42535200-7183-44b1-9745-464c91b6edbb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) new shower gel kine and humorous campaign “The Neighbour”.
	+ Axe rediscovering what made the brand cool and kind of corny.
* Hiscox [shocks](https://substack.com/redirect/a7e9e4b8-71c4-4599-8930-5e4b09104ab0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) by sending wine-stained mailer to customers.
	+ Best use of old-timey ad formats, mail advertising for the 2020s.
* Polaroid’s OOH ads [celebrate](https://substack.com/redirect/b6372d87-9aca-4bbb-83b3-5c1004529050?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the joy of real life in a world of digital overload and AI Slop.
	+ It might be the best OOH campaign of the year, if we go by the audience.
* Nike and Dutch Lionesses unveil ‘[Oranje Fatalis](https://substack.com/redirect/d4641069-c9f0-4186-b7f7-ed55cfc08f16?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ – A Tulip with Thorns.
	+ Best football activation of the year.

[![Outdoor ad from Polaroid's new campaign](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!XNTT!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F71ee64ca-32d2-4bcd-a6e2-835aa2b4575d_1920x1080.jpeg "Outdoor ad from Polaroid's new campaign")](https://substack.com/redirect/df34803e-955c-477c-8040-764c8816f267?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Waitrose & Partners [skewers](https://substack.com/redirect/09413309-89b0-45ca-a850-e68b497b95ae?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) summer campaign with [kebab](https://substack.com/redirect/b0ed282e-8ac1-4e18-b019-e5717262d709?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) special builds.
	+ Best Experimental OOH campaign of the year.
* [Ralph Lauren](https://substack.com/redirect/ca46256b-d860-40b6-bb55-cd2255a254cb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) paying [homage](https://substack.com/redirect/bde3ad6a-7ca1-4e05-8200-a21044d0c7f7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to generations of Black Martha’s Vineyard visitors in a new collection.
	+ It was perfect and Ralph Lauren did their best work last year.
* Gwyneth's [Astronomer](https://substack.com/redirect/48b99522-2508-4977-af85-7c4d2de30a37?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Video Stunt. 
	+ It’s alright, minimum effort.
* Rascals Premium Diapers went viral for their [TikTok response](https://substack.com/redirect/bd9927db-2897-45f7-b84e-70f3c1b05618?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to @shanellereeves.
	+ Best TikTok stitch brand response of the year.
* Rains “[Getting dressed should be fun](https://substack.com/redirect/4ac22e1d-115d-4a36-86d8-1a34035d637b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)”⁠ social campaign. 
	+ It’s giving fashion for the love of fashion and curation.
* [Wes Anderson](https://substack.com/redirect/d46387ff-7e51-4aad-b6f2-c7576f225f3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) was back for a second Montblanc campaign.
	+ Engaging and Beautiful.

Honourable mentions: Nathan’s Famous [NYC Subway](https://substack.com/redirect/3c18a919-eb4d-4c96-bc99-41372301f961?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) campaign, Income Insurance's [Max the Stress Ball](https://substack.com/redirect/673978f0-08a8-485d-8d86-5ebf88d2b55d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) campaign, McDonald’s Australia sticking [Sauce Lids to wall](https://substack.com/redirect/6390e89a-07c3-4820-9bb7-3e081a42e1ab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), Vattenfall x [Samuel L. Jackson](https://substack.com/redirect/f9e4297d-eed3-462a-b082-bfe5525bd3b0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) advert, Cash App x [Timotheé Chalamet](https://substack.com/redirect/deb5ac80-bef3-4225-8736-7b9dec0b2419?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), and Pure Leaf’s [vending machine](https://substack.com/redirect/f9ba9c00-7dc7-4753-a42b-365aa7d21b0f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

##### August 2025

* GAP x Katseye “[Better in Denim](https://substack.com/redirect/a2cd82e9-c3db-40dd-8d10-c587aef943bb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” campaign. 
	+ Best of Best 2025.
* In new campaign, Hornbach casts DIY chaos as a [choral odyssey](https://substack.com/redirect/8a71f10b-491d-4c75-98d0-bbccfc7fc934?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Best art direction of the year.
* Columbia slams ‘[pristine](https://substack.com/redirect/2f8c82ac-1cc6-457b-8c5a-528ed44782d8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ outdoors marketing with new “[Engineered for Whatever](https://substack.com/redirect/d0bd0328-343b-4992-98c5-ed8733914c9a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” brand platform.
	+ A brand rediscovering itself and scaring off performative buyers.
* McDonald’s '[Take a Trip Through McDonaldland](https://substack.com/redirect/390b8623-f535-4d23-8e09-a96c0395b6cf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)' campaign. 
	+ Best of 2025, according to people at Adweek.
* Cadbury’s “[Made to be enjoyed, not endured](https://substack.com/redirect/a2aebe38-bd42-40f2-ad4f-27f4de4afa68?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” dark chocolate campaign. 
	+ That’s what I call incredible use of product positioning and an invitation to new audiences to give your product and category a try.
* Stone Island \*[actually](https://substack.com/redirect/063999fb-a46b-4812-9922-d41ee08e3255?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)\* swam with sharks for its new campaign.
	+ Putting the stone in Stone Island and the island in fashion.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/youtube/w_728,c_limit/l_youtube_play_qyqt8q,w_120/IwzF26o0AuU)](https://substack.com/redirect/a2cd82e9-c3db-40dd-8d10-c587aef943bb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)

* Tilda Swinton fronts [Gentle Monster’s](https://substack.com/redirect/114b48be-f5cc-4c3c-96e0-01b66c0fb5df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) futuristic “BOLD” campaign.
	+ Best celebrity-led campaign in fashion.
* [W Magazine editorial campaign](https://substack.com/redirect/858a6c65-e221-40be-a5c9-597a0abc33d2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for this Louis Vuitton’s new product line-up.
	+ Second best in art direction, it could have been first, but I’m taking away a few points because it references another old campaign.
* Cheese with a message – and illustrations: [The Milram Design Edition](https://substack.com/redirect/8fb5b8dd-9903-42b9-b5b1-716cf28614d2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Best use of product packaging to highlight a brand purpose.
* Dior’s ‘[Diorling](https://substack.com/redirect/126a38d3-cb39-41db-a8f0-f8579701e17c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ campaign
	+ Is it the cutest advert of the year?
* Parents for Future UK’s “[Sorry](https://substack.com/redirect/0a8ed3b5-da26-4319-a9a4-19e49581cc5e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” campaign. 
	+ Best climate change campaign of the year.

Honourable mentions: Telstra x [Steve Buscemi](https://substack.com/redirect/30c0991f-edef-475d-af31-56c219ddfb61?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) network security campaign, [IKEA Brighton](https://substack.com/redirect/87a18be0-aa5b-4bab-9f76-b05741d1057a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) store-opening campaign, Channel 4’s “[Educating Yorkshire](https://substack.com/redirect/f68217b2-707c-4cc3-9063-45abb41eaf05?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” advert, Instyle Magazine’s “[The Intern](https://substack.com/redirect/7e42107f-eced-4d48-ab38-76c85d81a24b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” social series. 

##### September 2025

* Depop’s “[Where Taste Recognizes Taste](https://substack.com/redirect/b7f3b180-11dc-46bb-bb73-35fd8da5a251?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” campaign. 
	+ Best video campaign in retail fashion.
* Tootsie Pop’s ‘[How Many Licks](https://substack.com/redirect/2deb7043-d92d-420a-bfc4-17ff6a0a50b2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)‘ commercial getting a tiny [makeover](https://substack.com/redirect/d25c1da0-3a50-4848-bbe4-acec205f64bf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Best campaign revival/refresh of the year.
* Tyler, The Creator [narrating](https://substack.com/redirect/37134dc9-f0f8-4605-81af-e53e1d0f4b51?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Nike’s new "Why Do It" campaign.
	+ It’s good, but not the best, and dare I say it’s only good because of the timing.
* Humane Canada reacts to Sabrina Carpenter’s album launch with “[Dogs are Man’s True Best Friends](https://substack.com/redirect/69354420-12f6-481a-9209-81d4378e4efe?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” campaign.
	+ Best use of a cultural moment in Social + OOH campaign.
* The “[hold the dots and scrolls](https://substack.com/redirect/1b51fdc1-8369-40a7-a318-5e21b74e9017?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” instagram post from Gentle Monster.
	+ Best instagram campaign of the year.
* [Laphroaig x Willem Dafoe](https://substack.com/redirect/88414d3e-cfe4-4ce8-92d8-6665fd5750a0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is a peaty partnership and campaign worth savouring.
	+ Best artsy campaign of the year.
* Netflix’s "[The Thursday Murder Club](https://substack.com/redirect/3e4a61c1-ce27-45cb-a656-76f12180c658?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)" knitted OOH campaign. 
	+ We’ll see more of this soon-ish, as Red Tape did something similar last month.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!-psc!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fafdaaeae-c4af-433b-a4e1-15780fb3fb75_1176x550.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/99de5431-76e3-4f0e-9fcd-af3ee45b6c8b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Anthropic’s first major brand campaign for [Claude](https://substack.com/redirect/8a0dd98b-4682-4cfc-9ace-b204e35c88de?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), its AI assistant.
	+ Best campaign of the year in AI & Technology.
* Mercedes India celebrates dreamers in ‘[Dream Days](https://substack.com/redirect/dab5a9f7-32fe-40f6-b048-9eb0d5c4bf6b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ campaign.
	+ Best print campaign of the year.
* DID SOMEBODY SAY…CRAIG DAVID? Just Eat [unveils](https://substack.com/redirect/486ffc92-5786-485f-8bef-2df3f9adb64e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) music legend as first-ever UK face of its iconic ad.
	+ Best original musical campaign of the year.
* paloma wool’s [Autumn Winter 2025 campaign](https://substack.com/redirect/b26ba0a4-3c9b-4935-b77e-32e5e7458403?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). 
	+ The campaign that gives off similar vibes to Variety’s recent covers.
* Doja Cat eating ‘[Lipstick](https://substack.com/redirect/e3c7cd48-de2f-42ed-877b-f38d6eefc0a3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ at the MTV VMAs.
	+ I miss brand stunts on red carpets, the 2000s were full of PR hijacks.
* ZEISS campaign shows how sunlight [silently damages](https://substack.com/redirect/1a753c80-9b20-49bf-8766-944470274118?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) vision.
	+ Beautifully shot and short.
* Audi’s [OOH campaign](https://substack.com/redirect/dc943807-b69d-4f80-b394-8b97e602f7b9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) translates the intricate sound of Audiʼs RS models accelerating into sheet music.
	+ More brands should use OOH campaigns as visual clues and stories to build curiosity.
* Sam Altman’s World.org creating a [moving bot-board](https://substack.com/redirect/ff30fa51-4f3f-4f63-887a-497a50d8cf9e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), carried by three robots at Mahattan Beach, Los Angeles.
	+ They say this is the future of outdoor advertising.
* [Lurpak](https://substack.com/redirect/6213f1d9-f2fe-418c-8175-c56e6b9de7b6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) ‘Scones’, ‘Cookies’ and ‘Brownies’ packaging.
	+ Simplify everything until people want complexity.

Honourable mentions: G-STAR’s [Anatomic Denim](https://substack.com/redirect/07a9c2bb-2a57-4df8-ae38-86bbd49cb3ff?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) ad campaign, Dunkin’ “[Home](https://substack.com/redirect/8f58e8e0-b5e3-4936-ae97-7ca848989525?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” OOH advert, Lime’s ‘[Good Service on All Limes](https://substack.com/redirect/56bb5c29-987e-4680-92ee-55e818f7ed01?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ Tube strike ads, John Lewis [100 years anniversary](https://substack.com/redirect/af6ed316-6670-4c16-931a-c176cfe53991?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) campaign, and Essity’s [Tempo](https://substack.com/redirect/aed86d90-ac8c-4e8d-960d-1f721979fd0d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Europe-wide campaign.

##### October 2025

* Think Different 2.0: Jane Goodall has [one last collaboration](https://substack.com/redirect/757e06e8-5913-4253-b796-bf7accd86ea6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with Apple.
	+ What if, just like Netflix, Apple starts filming adverts with legends close to their deathbeds and releases them like Netflix releases exclusive interviews?
* Life360 is back with [another](https://substack.com/redirect/24d8f90f-2dd0-43e2-a1ee-5ba6ba84dd42?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) animated short film to remind everyone “[All the Best People are Dogs](https://substack.com/redirect/4233a124-9ab2-45f5-abf0-09b97b618b3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).”
	+ Best use of dogs in an advert.
* After his ‘Bachelor’-inspired ad spot, [Zohran Mamdani](https://substack.com/redirect/ad97ee27-b105-431c-a1de-8d4e74dd4d1f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is courting [another](https://substack.com/redirect/25942b69-55a1-40b3-8295-2ed799b53513?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) crucial reality-TV fandom.
	+ No explanation needed.
* Spotify’s [Tunetorials](https://substack.com/redirect/0d1f3c5e-a9f9-450b-9e86-cf1ba50f2de0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) brings advertising strategies to life with beats you won’t forget.
	+ Best B2B campaign of the year.
* [The Ordinary](https://substack.com/redirect/5995d9de-55d9-4402-a096-d05bc2bf5690?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) tackles ‘misleading’ beauty jargon with [The Periodic Fable](https://substack.com/redirect/5e64fd94-0b17-4d4b-9057-6a0ca316f972?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) campaign.
	+ Jargon is everywhere, and people are tired.
* Deutsche Bahn (German Rail) [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/2aac7880-5d2a-4dbf-9ddb-6c22071f2317?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) hilarious web series “[Boah, Bahn! We’re all in the same train](https://substack.com/redirect/550d2047-8ae1-47e6-83a7-95a4a40f832f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).”
	+ A sitcom-like content series is the remedy for brands currently struggling in their industry and facing customer complaints all the time.
* [Al Pacino and Robert De Niro](https://substack.com/redirect/83389e6b-bc85-440d-a82e-c5da66cf0875?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) snuggled up for a new Moncler campaign.
	+ It’s for the best, Moncler moving in the right direction.
* Chupps unveils [planet conscious billboard](https://substack.com/redirect/4e3f4d41-5059-406d-82da-bbf1fb1831e7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to raise awareness about fashion waste.
	+ Actually sustainable.
* MITH Bangkok marks its 10th anniversary with “[The Scent That Gives You The Scene](https://substack.com/redirect/de7507f0-ddbd-47d3-b088-b07c743c3c7c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g),” a hilarious + strategic campaign.
	+ Making the consumer your hero works again and again.
* The [short films](https://substack.com/redirect/814d3e44-567f-4878-aeec-e1f061060e29?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from ‘[It’s Always Burberry Weather: Postcards from London](https://substack.com/redirect/f21a2965-b5b4-45a8-a3f1-bbff8f117e6f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ campaign. 
	+ Brand heritage at play, icons being icons, and a bit stereotypical.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!ulfU!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F7dad2fe9-c496-4a30-8056-46f9c8ff23e7_2200x1118.jpeg)](https://substack.com/redirect/6d606ef7-c77d-4e1a-81d1-6be8cf9cc410?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Heineken drops [social spaces](https://substack.com/redirect/fc131795-0b34-4e9e-8bfe-9ba9b6b183a8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from space to tackle [urban isolation](https://substack.com/redirect/ab6a416c-6a5e-4a29-a304-6ea4852b8031?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Socialisation and Brand Colors.
* [KFC Canada](https://substack.com/redirect/3ddd5e4b-5e3a-4025-81ea-0610aa70cccd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) gives the [colonel](https://substack.com/redirect/b61b629e-f369-40fc-980c-896b59e149e8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) a Hockey season makeover.
	+ Not an award, but the best campaign in terms of a brand using a key regional sport and making fans and non-fans happy.
* Selleys have put their [famous Liquid Nails](https://substack.com/redirect/10d14f64-2219-46ec-8ee0-b082328118f5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to the ultimate test — by gluing a range of objects to a outdoor billboard and inviting the public to try and take them.
	+ I loved the formatting and selection of objects in the billboard.
* Hatch turns the [horrors of doomscrolling](https://substack.com/redirect/b3d0bd09-6391-4b5d-a4ae-20f2ac09c624?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) into a scary movie trailer.
	+ Best Halloween campaign of the year.
* Hermès “Horse & Cart” campaign, directed by [Tom Emmerson](https://substack.com/redirect/0168c3e2-eec2-48d8-9aa5-9aaea3af2ccf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Just wanted to include something from Hermès, as 2025 was their year.
* [Dunkin](https://substack.com/redirect/84b6c130-fc40-40df-9bbe-d894e575562c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) wins the early Halloween marketing game with this social post and other content on their profile.
	+ Best social post of the year, according to LinkedIn pundits.
* ZARA [releases](https://substack.com/redirect/49112ed1-ad9d-404e-aef6-6f48f606d6a0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Halloween 2025 Collection, shot by Szilveszter Mako.
	+ A fast fashion campaign that makes you rethink luxury.
* German Agency Böcker’s ‘[Quiet As A Whisper](https://substack.com/redirect/5679b108-2794-42cb-a546-88e9979122e4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ launches [witty campaign](https://substack.com/redirect/1aedbe96-882f-4b6e-9b71-eb187d71cbd1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after its lift used in Louvre Heist.
	+ Best reactive marketing campaign of the year.
* [British Airways ads](https://substack.com/redirect/335a01ad-feb4-4809-9957-7a91773c3073?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) capture the wonder of travel from the air and serves the brand message through “[Reflections](https://substack.com/redirect/335a01ad-feb4-4809-9957-7a91773c3073?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).”
	+ A nightmare for the C-suite and the “make the logo bigger” audience.
* Heineken’s [reaction](https://substack.com/redirect/4c7c87be-eedd-49e1-adf9-3adb544e93c0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to Friend.com marketing campaign and product. 
	+ Campaign vs. Campaign.
* [Ramp’s big stunt](https://substack.com/redirect/83d9cf04-ee55-4403-b253-64f7fd913045?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and wholesome [marketing](https://substack.com/redirect/37a0b74e-f065-4c38-bafa-66a16ff75755?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Best brand pop-up of the year.
* [Down Under Film Festival’s](https://substack.com/redirect/e250cc01-7213-4bf7-a189-ea328bc94e4c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) promotional film “[Holy Crocodile!](https://substack.com/redirect/afdf3f50-a5d8-4c2c-bc94-e831c7bb4329?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” is perfect and promotes global creativity.
	+ Best event marketing campaign of the year.
* Spotify's “[Fan Life](https://substack.com/redirect/3cb56c26-1976-4b24-a434-e007237429b3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” campaign.
	+ Best ‘vibe sells’ campaign of the year.

Honourable mentions: Breast Cancer UK’s [pavements](https://substack.com/redirect/bb8f3a23-6d2b-47d3-b918-030f22768a1e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) campaign, O2 ‘[Near or Far](https://substack.com/redirect/275a7105-6106-405a-85f4-af5bf7f7a7cd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ campaign, Uber Australia’s “Can’t do that if you’re driving” advert, and [European Wax Center](https://substack.com/redirect/63846d96-ff34-40a8-813d-9a78a6141b2a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) reacting to [SKIMS’s viral](https://substack.com/redirect/fac9a141-75bc-4389-b7ab-e1fac004628b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) bush thong.

##### November 2025

* PlayStation 5 brings the [unexpected](https://substack.com/redirect/3a602768-a63b-46ae-ba6f-a9e7591ff155?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to [life](https://substack.com/redirect/38705935-1889-478f-8e48-0a89a33c1de3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with ‘[It Happens on PlayStation 5](https://substack.com/redirect/f391d161-8cff-4a77-a823-a3b7fda71d61?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ campaign.
	+ Best global campaign of the year.
* Ikea lets its [price tags tell the story](https://substack.com/redirect/ff44e427-f7e6-4060-ab7b-e38278636af3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in OOH campaign.
	+ It’s steamy and cute.
* Stephen Curry shoots the moon in [L.A. billboard stunt](https://substack.com/redirect/6ebd3515-2d01-4d0a-a1cf-fdedd5895163?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for his new book.
	+ A meme turns into a billboard, the moon is done, let’s do clouds for AWS.
* Formula 1 and [LEGO](https://substack.com/redirect/60af0e01-a1dc-4085-8e31-f97e64065e4b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) bring [extra flair](https://substack.com/redirect/87f0de7b-c8d0-41cb-b307-d9ecd5e88f02?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to the Las Vegas Grand Prix 2025.
	+ Best F1 activation of the year and use of Las Vegas Sphere.
* Heinz turning [tomatoes into art](https://substack.com/redirect/24dfd7e9-8b29-46a2-9dad-92093730bc3e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for China’s national games. 
	+ Best sports-focused campaign of the year from non-sport brand.
* In U.K. Charity’s holiday PSA campaign, ‘[Total Eclipse of the Heart](https://substack.com/redirect/ed7df23d-1556-4d2d-b510-6975947652b3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ takes on deeper meaning.
	+ Best PSA Christmas campaign of the year.
* [Nike’s Soup Shop](https://substack.com/redirect/2d2fb8f7-bd0f-4eab-97fe-04d9dc2c197d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): How a pop-up won Gen Z in Guangzhou.
	+ We finally got sports brands to embrace some elements of Asian food culture.

[![It Happens on PS5 - ad campaign](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!GW06!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F9aebe5b6-e693-4644-b6aa-9301643b5bf4_8192x4184.jpeg "It Happens on PS5 - ad campaign")](https://substack.com/redirect/bbccf59e-0601-4c6b-acd6-d4ed04e143ac?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Jacquemus launches “[Le Valèrie](https://substack.com/redirect/840f53c9-7dfd-4be0-b37b-657872b53130?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)”, a handbag dedicated to his mother, and the campaign for this launch has become [everyone’s favourite](https://substack.com/redirect/8ed1ca55-9953-405d-b79d-f03a0c18b086?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ This is a marketing-first brand, and it should stay that way.
* [Timothee Chalamet](https://substack.com/redirect/48f401e6-299c-4cd2-aa18-1a0ed7740b45?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) wasn’t kidding: “[Marty Supreme](https://substack.com/redirect/e969886b-1dbf-4b51-902a-2748abe44238?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” marketing onslaught [includes](https://substack.com/redirect/133405dc-4f56-4701-ae79-a5357482b6e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Real Orange Blimp, Virtual Ones on Google.
	+ Best movie marketing campaign of the year, according to…
* The full page [Nike ad](https://substack.com/redirect/0d200d01-4316-4a50-8060-45aa77556d45?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in the NYT.
	+ The [OOH campaign](https://substack.com/redirect/69ee6b4c-115b-44b1-aaf2-a275f85cfcbc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in NYC was also so good.
* JOY! Collective and Farrow & Ball bring [London’s streets to life](https://substack.com/redirect/30c9bcfb-7b39-4d21-b48f-937692359d61?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with hyper-real mural campaign.
	+ Best Mural campaign of the year.
* Hasbro's first official luxury [Jenga collab](https://substack.com/redirect/9d9c55ec-5abd-4234-b407-58476a379470?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with Bottega Veneta. 
	+ It’s performative and luxury.

Honourable mentions: [Bradley Cooper](https://substack.com/redirect/6e2bc35b-5565-4a9c-b0b1-eababa37ef27?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) x Uber Eats’ Football Conspiracy, Gentle Monster short film ‘[THE HUNT](https://substack.com/redirect/9dd3a3ff-f5b4-4570-8997-22011023232f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g),’ and Ford’s “[We’re here for the work that makes America work](https://substack.com/redirect/1319c1e8-5981-40c8-af66-fb2d4b5e5cd9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” print campaign. 

##### December 2025

* The Simpsons star in ‘[Santa’s Little Helper](https://substack.com/redirect/05ce09bf-1c3d-4852-a99b-85eb165da37e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ for NZ’s Greyhound rehoming campaign.
	+ Best use of IP in PSA campaign.
* Icelandair claims [Iceland is real](https://substack.com/redirect/76d3e232-e215-42ef-a1fd-c57aaddb1b8a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and not AI-generated.
	+ Best tourism campaign of the year.
* Flair-filled Nike film celebrates [Africa’s football heritage](https://substack.com/redirect/48ef9420-dcbd-41f5-8f1d-a60ef164e59f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Best football campaign of the year.
* Intermarché’s ‘[Unloved](https://substack.com/redirect/397c97a8-e587-4747-b517-84497aa57c9e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ turns a wolf into a Christmas hero.
	+ Best Christmas ad of the year.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!K7_d!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1533756b-2ee3-4674-9f37-4c82868948f8_860x485.jpeg)](https://substack.com/redirect/bd7dfd98-a442-478c-a836-33c7c390c911?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Embark on a musical journey through Switzerland with [Swiss Sounds](https://substack.com/redirect/7b916131-4a45-483d-ba7d-b6ce5cbde207?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), the crazy project by Thylacine and Switzerland Tourism.
	+ It’s time we stop selling the vibe and embrace the sound, more audio-only campaigns in 2026.
* ‘[Im Not Remarkable](https://substack.com/redirect/81229a54-e7ce-4cfd-ac95-20d00514da46?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ – Apple’s inspiring musical film [highlights](https://substack.com/redirect/9836a413-5f75-4cff-a232-1a1d34bba1eb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) accessibility features.
	+ We need more accessibility-focused campaigns in advertising.
* Canal+ promotes [6-month cinema window](https://substack.com/redirect/8b2d2879-6719-418c-916e-229e8427eb31?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with ‘[Already on Canal+](https://substack.com/redirect/760462ea-6194-4212-97fa-727d6c448e32?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ campaign.
	+ Best use of platform UI in an OOH campaign.
* A fountain pouring free [Peroni Nastro Azzurro](https://substack.com/redirect/2c237431-0592-4282-a6f2-a69b646fcebd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) popped up on [South Bank](https://substack.com/redirect/11d51df0-5251-4c1b-90fe-687923aa4c61?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Whatever works.
* LiveOnNY’s [organ donation](https://substack.com/redirect/78d81028-174b-4a4a-bcc4-3221e51e785c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) campaign in the Subway. 
	+ Best transit campaign of the year.
* Porsche’s “[A coded love letter](https://substack.com/redirect/7fab4096-189a-4e8e-938a-8baee4a47a7d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” campaign.
	+ It’s anti-AI marketing\_\_?

Honourable mentions: Red Wing’s “[Made the Hard Way](https://substack.com/redirect/1600fee5-9597-4795-afa5-a0d5224c934e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” OOH campaign, Loveholidays takes [Joe Marler](https://substack.com/redirect/e80813ff-132e-4b9d-a980-ca22dc50af99?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on a sunny holiday inside a billboard, Beats by Dre x [ishowspeed](https://substack.com/redirect/e6797e9c-ca48-45b2-8ed5-2bba0b3ea4d3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) ad, and Safehaven’s [Giving Tuesday](https://substack.com/redirect/82231301-422e-4d31-8a7b-660b6d03f8a3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) print advert.

* [Yard NYC](https://substack.com/redirect/c74ee928-8a20-49bb-bcda-4e191f466439?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) appointed as brand agency for European Wax Center.
* [R/GA](https://substack.com/redirect/63f45583-9516-4280-9870-a1a125083af7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is bringing TurboTax Back to the Super Bowl.

The Social Juice - Best of Marketing on Instagram: "What’s in i…

***The Social Juice+** includes access to newsletter archive and strategy newsletters. **It’s your decision, But you are losing all three benefits**. When you can also expense it:* [Generate](https://substack.com/redirect/30f3757d-b627-4d10-a088-20f550304c74?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) an Employee Expense Email for Your Manager.

*\* **Others:** [Student Discount](https://substack.com/redirect/daf270dd-96f9-4ff4-b02b-e753def70ebf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) & Localised pricing for [Asia](https://substack.com/redirect/c27296cb-ad98-4ce9-a300-65feade62b87?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), [Eastern Europe](https://substack.com/redirect/20638549-51d4-4ddb-a43a-d9129dee4737?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), [South America](https://substack.com/redirect/47859284-7fe9-435d-bbbd-d8e3c85de554?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) & [Africa](https://substack.com/redirect/b03fd777-7108-4c6a-b2ac-17549bb33a21?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).*

<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;"><span>This newsletter is sponsored by our friends at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/976b2e89-5486-4e2c-b1bc-c3d450662f00?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span>, the always-on, affordable brand tracking dashboard. Now tracking 25 markets (and growing), giving you consistent brand health data everywhere you operate.</span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you’ll get dividends for years.</p></blockquote>
